Ka "Monday" : Ka "Sngi Hat

ICa ‘Monday*’ no "Sngi Hat Balaf*. 

Ha kiba bun ki kam mynta te la iaid hi da ki 12 (khad*ar) 
bnai ha ka shisnexn na u January baduh December Nangta la 
pyndonkam ia u Kyllalyr.gkot hadtih Nohprah ryngkat bad ktwei 
pat ki 10 (shiphew) ki bnai ba la jer kyrieng ban ia pyn- 
biang bad kifei ki 12 ^had-ar) bnai ba la jer kyrteng ter-ter na- 
duh January haduh December. Shaphang ka jiugmut jong ki kvr- 
teng naduh January baduh Deceitober te ki la shu sah bi tung ki 
kyrteng bad la pyndonkam la kita ki kvrleng ban suk ban tip 
ban kynmaw ha ki kam ki jam ha ka trei ka ktah bad ha kaba

ki blei ki dken. bynrei baba la lai pyndonkam te lehse ym doa 
daw shnb ban dang sngewkbobhioh ba kaba pyndonkam ia ki.

(hynniew) sngi ha ka sbitaiew na ka Sunday baduh ka Saturday

(bd la la pyndonkam paidbah bha mynta Kum ka Sngi Saitiain).

donkam te Jehsc ym don daw shuh ban dang la si/gewkhohhioh

U Juh U Bnai Ha Sahit Bneng 

Ka Jaidbynriaw Bunkur lane Hynniewlrep ka tip ban khein 
ia ka jingkut jong ka Snem bad jingsdang jong ka Snem. Haba 
ahu kren kyllam ka Snem Kbasi ka sdang nadub ka sngi kaba 
bud ia ka miet kaba jrong tam (Longest Night), U bnai ('Nai 
bneng) uba don ha ka miet kaba jrong tam (Longest Night) u 
dei u ’Nai Nobprah. Ka jingwan j’ong ka British Government 
ryngkat bad ka Gregorian Calendar ka la sain dur ia ka rukom 
khein bnai, bad la sbu ia pynbiang jubor ha ki 12 (khad ar) tylli 
tki bnai pbareng (naduh sbi tank January hadub 3t tarik Dec* 
ember), Kbam bunslen u bnai u mih (New Moon Day) 12 (khad 
ar) sien bapoh ka Snem, bynrei hateng-bateng u mih 13 (khadSai) 
bad haba ka long kumta, la uba khad ar ia tip nob kum u 
’Nai Sbonglad, bad uba khadlai u sab hi kum u 'Nai 
Nobprah. Ia u 'Nai Nobprah la pyobynta ha ki ar der (period/, 
kaba nyngkong ka dei naduh ba u mih (New Moon Day) ha* 
dub ka miet kaba jrong tam (Longest Night), bad ka der (per*

iod) kaba ar ka dei nadub ka miet kaba jrong tam (Longest 
Night) hadub ba mib (New Moon Day) u Nai Kyllalyngkot Ha 
ka jingkhein snem la shim ba u 'Nai Kyllalyngkot u dei u bnai 
Jiba nyngkong. Nangta bud ler*ler u ’Nai Rympbang, ‘Nai Lber,
